    import Link from "next/link";
    import { Suspense } from "react";
    import { notFound } from "next/navigation";
    import { requireArea } from "@/lib/rbac";
    import { ServerTags } from "@/components/ui/ServerTags";
    import { loadOwnershipTags } from "@/lib/server-ownership-tags-data";
    import { StatusDot, Badge } from "@/components/ui/primitives";
    import { PanelSkeleton } from "@/components/admin/shell/primitives";
    import { AutoRefresh } from "@/components/AutoRefresh";
    import { REGION_LABELS } from "@/lib/node-regions";
    import { isGameFamily } from "@/lib/game-control";
    import { resolveServerTab, serverTabsFor } from "@/lib/admin-server/tabs";
    import { TabBar } from "@/components/admin/server360/TabBar";
    import { loadServerCore, gameFamilyOf } from "@/components/admin/server360/core";
    import { OverviewTab } from "@/components/admin/server360/tabs/OverviewTab";
    import { NetworkTab } from "@/components/admin/server360/tabs/NetworkTab";
    import { AccessTab } from "@/components/admin/server360/tabs/AccessTab";
    import { ComputeTab } from "@/components/admin/server360/tabs/ComputeTab";
    import { SoftwareTab } from "@/components/admin/server360/tabs/SoftwareTab";
    import { FilesTab } from "@/components/admin/server360/tabs/FilesTab";
    import { BackupsTab } from "@/components/admin/server360/tabs/BackupsTab";
    import { GameTab } from "@/components/admin/server360/tabs/GameTab";
    import { MoveTab } from "@/components/admin/server360/tabs/MoveTab";
    import { BillingTab } from "@/components/admin/server360/tabs/BillingTab";
    import { ActivityTab } from "@/components/admin/server360/tabs/ActivityTab";
    import { DangerTab } from "@/components/admin/server360/tabs/DangerTab";
    
    export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";
    
    const TRANSITIONAL = ["PENDING", "PROVISIONING", "REINSTALLING", "DELETING"];
    
     
    export default async function AdminServerDetail({
      params,
      searchParams,
    }: {
      params: Promise<{ id: string }>;
      searchParams: Promise<{ tab?: string }>;
    }) {
      await requireArea("fleet");
      const { id } = await params;
      const sp = await searchParams;
      const server = await loadServerCore(id);
      if (!server) notFound();
    
      const ownershipTags = (await loadOwnershipTags([server])).get(server.id) ?? [];
      const family = gameFamilyOf(server);
      const isGame = isGameFamily(family);
      const tab = resolveServerTab(sp.tab, isGame);
      const tabs = serverTabsFor(isGame).map((t) => ({ key: t.key, label: t.label, danger: "danger" in t ? t.danger : false }));
      const activeJob = server.jobs[0] ?? null;
      const planName = server.gamePlan ? server.gamePlan.name : server.plan.name;
      const base = `/admin/servers/${server.id}`;
    
      return (
        <div className="space-y-5">
          {(activeJob || TRANSITIONAL.includes(server.status)) && <AutoRefresh />}
    
          <header className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-4">
            <div className="min-w-0">
              <p className="adm-eyebrow mb-1.5">{isGame ? "Game server" : "VPS"} · {server.vmid ? `VMID ${server.vmid}` : "no VM yet"}</p>
              <h1 className="page-title flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 break-all">
                {server.label || server.hostname}
                <StatusDot status={server.status} />
                <ServerTags tags={ownershipTags} />
                {server.dedicatedIp && <Badge tone="brand">dedicated IP</Badge>}
              </h1>
              <p className="mt-1 text-sm" style={{ color: "var(--adm-muted)" }}>
                {server.label && <span className="font-mono">{server.hostname} · </span>}
                {planName} · {server.node ? `${server.node.name} (${REGION_LABELS[server.node.region] ?? server.node.region})` : "no node"} ·{" "}
                owner{" "}
                <Link href={`/admin/users/${server.user.id}`} className="text-brand-300 hover:underline">
                  {server.user.email}
                </Link>
              </p>
            </div>
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
              {activeJob && (
                <span className="adm-pill" data-tone="blue" title={activeJob.step ?? undefined}>
                  {activeJob.type.toLowerCase().replace(/_/g, " ")} · {activeJob.status.toLowerCase()}
                  {activeJob.progress > 0 ? ` ${activeJob.progress}%` : ""}
                </span>
              )}
              <Link href={`/dashboard/servers/${server.id}`} className="btn-ghost py-1.5 text-xs" title="Opens the customer panel for this server as yourself (admin view, not impersonation)">
                Customer view ↗
              </Link>
              <Link href={`/admin/ops/servers/${server.id}`} className="btn-secondary py-1.5 text-xs">
                Diagnose
              </Link>
            </div>
          </header>
    
          {server.suspendedNote && (
            <div className="rounded-xl border border-amber-500/30 bg-amber-500/10 px-4 py-2.5 text-sm text-amber-300" role="status">
              Suspended{server.suspendReason ? ` (${server.suspendReason.toLowerCase()})` : ""}: {server.suspendedNote}
            </div>
          )}
    
          <TabBar
            base={base}
            tabs={tabs}
            active={tab}
            label="Server sections"
            badges={{ network: server._count.portForwards || undefined, activity: activeJob ? "1" : undefined }}
          />
    
          <Suspense key={tab} fallback={<div className="grid gap-4 xl:grid-cols-2"><PanelSkeleton rows={5} /><PanelSkeleton rows={5} /></div>}>
            {tab === "overview" && <OverviewTab server={server} isGame={isGame} />}
            {tab === "network" && <NetworkTab server={server} isGame={isGame} />}
            {tab === "access" && <AccessTab server={server} />}
            {tab === "compute" && <ComputeTab server={server} />}
            {tab === "software" && <SoftwareTab server={server} isGame={isGame} />}
            {tab === "files" && <FilesTab server={server} />}
            {tab === "backups" && <BackupsTab server={server} />}
            {tab === "game" && isGame && <GameTab server={server} />}
            {tab === "move" && <MoveTab server={server} />}
            {tab === "billing" && <BillingTab server={server} />}
            {tab === "activity" && <ActivityTab server={server} />}
            {tab === "danger" && <DangerTab server={server} isGame={isGame} />}
          </Suspense>
        </div>
      );
    }

    enum VmMoveStatus {
      PLANNED // dry-run finished, or a real move queued but not yet started
      RUNNING
      DONE // cutover committed; source VM kept STOPPED until VM_MOVE_CLEANUP
      ROLLED_BACK // a phase before commit failed; source running again, target removed
      FAILED // rollback itself did not fully succeed — needs a human (see `error`)
      CLEANED // source VM destroyed by VM_MOVE_CLEANUP
    }

      reason          String?
      requestedById   String?
      plan            Json?
      checkpoints     Json?
      log             Json?
      error           String?
    
    
      addressChanged  Boolean      @default(false)
      keepSourceUntil DateTime?
      sourceCleanedAt DateTime?
      startedAt       DateTime?
      finishedAt      DateTime?
      createdAt       DateTime     @default(now())
      updatedAt       DateTime     @updatedAt
    
      @@index([serverId])
      @@index([status])
    }

    enum HostingTaskKind {
      PHP_SWITCH
      WORDPRESS_INSTALL
      APP_INSTALL
      BACKUP
      RESTORE
    
      SUSPEND // disable vhost + FPM pool + lock the unix user (reversible)
      UNSUSPEND // reverse a SUSPEND
      TERMINATE // remove vhost/FPM, delete the jailed user + home, drop scoped DBs
    
      CREATE_SUBDOMAIN // mkdir a docroot + write/enable a <label>.<primary> vhost
      DELETE_SUBDOMAIN // reverse: remove the vhost + docroot
      CREATE_ADDON_DOMAIN // full external domain: own docroot + vhost
      DELETE_ADDON_DOMAIN // reverse: remove the vhost + docroot
      CREATE_CRON // (re)write the jailed user's crontab from its ACTIVE cron rows
      DELETE_CRON // remove a cron row + rewrite the crontab
    
    
      INSTALL_DBTOOL
    
    
      ENSURE_DBMGR
    
    
      APPLY_PLAN_LIMITS
    
    
      RESET_DB_PASSWORD
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
      SET_PRIMARY_DOMAIN
    
    
    
    
      CREATE_FTP_USER
    
      DELETE_FTP_USER
    
      RESET_FTP_PASSWORD
    
    
    
      CAPTURE_SITE_THUMB
    }
    
    enum HostingTaskStatus {
      PENDING
      RUNNING
      DONE
      FAILED
    }
    
    
    
    
    model HostingTask {
      id        String            @id @default(cuid())
      accountId String
      account   HostingAccount    @relation(fields: [accountId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
      kind      HostingTaskKind
      status    HostingTaskStatus @default(PENDING)
      payload   String? // JSON in
      result    String? // JSON out (may hold a one-time secret until read)
      error     String?
      createdAt DateTime          @default(now())
      updatedAt DateTime          @updatedAt
    
      @@index([status])
      @@index([accountId])
    }
    
    model HostingBackup {

      @@index([accountId])

    model Card {

      comments    CardComment[]
    
      @@index([boardId])
      @@index([columnId])
    }
    
    model CardAttachment {

      cardId     String
      card       Card           @relation(fields: [cardId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
      filename   String // original name shown to the user
      storedName String // on-disk name (uuid + ext) under the board upload dir

      sizeBytes  Int            @default(0)
      kind       AttachmentKind @default(FILE)
      createdAt  DateTime       @default(now())
    
      @@index([cardId])
    }

    model GameMod {
      id               String      @id @default(cuid())
      gameFamily       String // minecraft, rust, valheim, cs2, ark-ase, ark-asa, palworld, terraria, project-zomboid, ...
      name             String

      category         String // Admin, Protection, Economy, Performance, Content, QoL, ...
    
    
    
    
      source           String
      sourceId         String? // workshop id / curseforge project id / modrinth slug / etc.
      url              String?
      modStringId      String? // e.g. Project Zomboid's Mods= string id (distinct from the numeric Workshop id)
      appliesToVariant String? // restrict to a loader/variant (e.g. "paper", "oxide", "carbon"); null = any
      description      String?
      iconUrl          String?
      popularity       Int         @default(0) // higher = more popular (sort key)
      paid             Boolean     @default(false)
      featured         Boolean     @default(false)
      active           Boolean     @default(true)
      createdAt        DateTime    @default(now())
      updatedAt        DateTime    @updatedAt
      installs         ServerMod[]
    
    
    
    
    
    
      gameVersions    String[]  @default([])
    
      loaders         String[]  @default([])
    
      downloads       Int?
    
      fileBytes       Int?

      serverSide      String?
    
      impact          String    @default("light")
    
      impactNote      String?
    
      verifiedAt      DateTime?
    
    
      upstreamOk      Boolean   @default(true)
    
    
      upstreamNote    String?
    
    
    
    
    
    
      license         String?

      authorName      String?
    
    
      authorAvatarUrl String?
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
      longDescription String?   @db.Text
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
      upstreamUpdatedAt DateTime?
    
    
      sourceUrl       String?
    
      licenseUrl      String?
    
    
      redistributable Boolean      @default(false)
    
      categories      String[]     @default([])
      tags            String[]     @default([])
    
    
      iconKey         String?
      galleryKeys     String[]     @default([])
    
      bodyHtml        String?      @db.Text
    
      latestVersion   String?
      followers       Int?
    
      syncedAt        DateTime?
      versionRows     ModVersion[]
    
    
      websiteUrl       String?
      issuesUrl        String?
      wikiUrl          String?
      discordUrl       String?
      donationUrl      String?
    
      rating           Float?
      ratingCount      Int?
    
      firstPublishedAt DateTime?
    
      clientSide       String?
    
      bodyFormat       String?
    
      authors          Json?
    
    
      duplicateOfId    String?
    
      dedupeKey        String?

      ratings ModRating[]

      @@index([source, sourceId])
      @@index([gameFamily, category])
      @@index([upstreamUpdatedAt])
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
      @@index([featured(sort: Desc), downloads(sort: Desc), popularity(sort: Desc), name])
      @@index([downloads(sort: Desc), name])
      @@index([createdAt(sort: Desc)])
      @@index([dedupeKey])
      @@index([duplicateOfId])
      @@index([gameFamily, source])
    }

    export async function register(
      prev: any,
      formData: FormData,
    ): Promise<{ error?: string }> {
      const parsed = credsSchema
        .extend({
          name: z.string().min(1, "Enter your name").max(100, "Name is too long"),
          password: z
            .string()
            .min(8, "Password must be at least 8 characters")
            .max(200, "Password is too long")
            .regex(/[a-zA-Z]/, "Password must include a letter")
            .regex(/[0-9]/, "Password must include a number"),
        })
        .safeParse({
    
    
        email: normalizeEmail(formData.get("email")),
        password: formData.get("password"),
        name: formData.get("name"),
      });
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
      if (!parsed.success) return { error: parsed.error.issues[0]?.message ?? "Check the form and try again." };
      const { email, password, name } = parsed.data;
    
    
      const regIp = clientIp(await headers());
      const regRl = checkRateLimit(`register:${email}:${regIp}`, { max: 5, windowMs: 15 * 60 * 1000 });
      if (!regRl.ok) return { error: `Too many attempts. Try again in ${regRl.retryAfterSec}s.` };
    
      const existing = await db.user.findUnique({ where: { email } });
      if (existing) return { error: "An account with that email already exists." };
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
      const jar = await cookies();
      const refCode = jar.get(REF_COOKIE)?.value?.trim().toUpperCase();
      let referredById: string | null = null;
      let isPartnerCode = false;
      let partnerCode: string | null = null;
      if (refCode) {
    
    
    
        const partner = await db.referralPartner.findUnique({
          where: { code: refCode },
          select: { status: true },
        });
        if (partner && partner.status === "ACTIVE") {
          isPartnerCode = true;
          partnerCode = refCode;
        } else {
          const referrer = await db.user.findUnique({ where: { referralCode: refCode } });
          if (referrer) referredById = referrer.id;
        }
      }
    
      const referralCode = await uniqueReferralCode();
      const user = await db.user.create({
        data: {
          email,
          name,
          passwordHash: await bcrypt.hash(password, 12),
          referralCode,
          referredById,
        },
      });
      await audit(
        user.id,
        "user.register",
        referredById ? `ref=${refCode}` : partnerCode ? `partner=${partnerCode}` : undefined,
      );
    
    
    
    
      try {
        if (isPartnerCode && partnerCode) {
          await attachCustomerToPartnerByCode(user.id, partnerCode);
        }
      } catch (e) {
        console.error("[register] partner attach failed for", user.id, e);
      }
      await createSession(user.id);
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
      try {
        await grantSignupCredit(user.id);
      } catch (e) {
        console.error("[register] signup credit grant failed for", user.id, e);
      }
      jar.delete(REF_COOKIE);
      try {
        const { sendMail, emails } = await import("./mailer");
        const tpl = emails.welcome(name);
        await sendMail({ to: email, subject: tpl.subject, html: tpl.html, userId: user.id, kind: "welcome" });
      } catch {  }
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
      let signupEventId = "";
      try {
        const h = await headers();
        const { eventId } = await sendMetaConversion({
          eventName: "CompleteRegistration",
          email,
          externalId: user.id,
          clientIp: clientIp(h),
          userAgent: h.get("user-agent") ?? undefined,
          fbc: jar.get("_fbc")?.value || undefined,
          fbp: jar.get("_fbp")?.value || undefined,
          sourceUrl: process.env.APP_URL ? `${process.env.APP_URL}/register` : undefined,
        });
        signupEventId = eventId;
      } catch {
        
      }
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
      try {
        const { sendVerificationEmail } = await import("./email-verification");
        await sendVerificationEmail(user);
      } catch (e) {
        console.error("[register] verification email failed:", e);
      }

        const { recordAdsConversion } = await import("./google-ads");
        await recordAdsConversion({ kind: "signup", userId: user.id, dedupeKey: `signup:${user.id}` });
      } catch {
        
      }

      const dest = next ?? "/dashboard";
      const marker = `signup=1${signupEventId ? `&se=${encodeURIComponent(signupEventId)}` : ""}`;
      redirect(`${dest}${dest.includes("?") ? "&" : "?"}${marker}`);
    }

  error: |-
    These are NETWORK-CALLABLE ENDPOINTS with no session check inside them.
    Every export of a file whose first line is "use server" gets a registered
    action id that anyone can POST to, with arguments of their choosing, with no
    session. A check in the page that renders the form is not a check.
    Either add a guard INSIDE the function (requireUser / requireAdmin /
    requireArea / an ownedX helper that proves the caller owns the id), or move
    the function to a plain module, or — if it is genuinely meant to be public —
    add it to ANONYMOUS_BY_DESIGN above with the reason.

    async function uniqueReferralCode(): Promise<string> {
      for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
        const code = generateReferralCode();
        const clash = await db.user.findUnique({ where: { referralCode: code } });
        if (!clash) return code;
      }
    
      return generateReferralCode(12);
    }

    async function grantSignupCredit(userId: string): Promise<number> {
    
    
      const cents = await getSettingInt("signup_free_credit_cents", signupFreeCreditCents());
      if (cents <= 0) return 0;
    
    
    
      const claimed = await db.user.updateMany({
        where: { id: userId, signupCreditCents: 0 },
        data: { balanceCents: { increment: cents }, signupCreditCents: cents },
      });
      if (claimed.count === 0) return 0;
      await db.walletTransaction.create({
        data: { userId, amountCents: cents, description: "Welcome credit" },
      });
      return cents;
    }

    const credsSchema = z.object({
      email: z.string().max(200).email("Enter a valid email address"),
      password: z.string().min(8).max(200),
    });
    
    export async function register(
      prev: any,
      formData: FormData,
    ): Promise<{ error?: string }> {
      const parsed = credsSchema
        .extend({
          name: z.string().min(1, "Enter your name").max(100, "Name is too long"),
          password: z
            .string()
            .min(8, "Password must be at least 8 characters")
            .max(200, "Password is too long")
            .regex(/[a-zA-Z]/, "Password must include a letter")
            .regex(/[0-9]/, "Password must include a number"),
        })
        .safeParse({
    
    
        email: normalizeEmail(formData.get("email")),
        password: formData.get("password"),
        name: formData.get("name"),
      });
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
      if (!parsed.success) return { error: parsed.error.issues[0]?.message ?? "Check the form and try again." };
      const { email, password, name } = parsed.data;
    
    
      const regIp = clientIp(await headers());
      const regRl = checkRateLimit(`register:${email}:${regIp}`, { max: 5, windowMs: 15 * 60 * 1000 });
      if (!regRl.ok) return { error: `Too many attempts. Try again in ${regRl.retryAfterSec}s.` };
    
      const existing = await db.user.findUnique({ where: { email } });
      if (existing) return { error: "An account with that email already exists." };
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
      const jar = await cookies();
      const refCode = jar.get(REF_COOKIE)?.value?.trim().toUpperCase();
      let referredById: string | null = null;
      let isPartnerCode = false;
      let partnerCode: string | null = null;
      if (refCode) {
    
    
    
        const partner = await db.referralPartner.findUnique({
          where: { code: refCode },
          select: { status: true },
        });
        if (partner && partner.status === "ACTIVE") {
          isPartnerCode = true;
          partnerCode = refCode;
        } else {
          const referrer = await db.user.findUnique({ where: { referralCode: refCode } });
          if (referrer) referredById = referrer.id;
        }
      }
    
      const referralCode = await uniqueReferralCode();
      const user = await db.user.create({
        data: {
          email,
          name,
          passwordHash: await bcrypt.hash(password, 12),
          referralCode,
          referredById,
        },
      });
      await audit(
        user.id,
        "user.register",
        referredById ? `ref=${refCode}` : partnerCode ? `partner=${partnerCode}` : undefined,
      );
    
    
    
    
      try {
        if (isPartnerCode && partnerCode) {
          await attachCustomerToPartnerByCode(user.id, partnerCode);
        }
      } catch (e) {
        console.error("[register] partner attach failed for", user.id, e);
      }
      await createSession(user.id);
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
      try {
        await grantSignupCredit(user.id);
      } catch (e) {
        console.error("[register] signup credit grant failed for", user.id, e);
      }
      jar.delete(REF_COOKIE);
      try {
        const { sendMail, emails } = await import("./mailer");
        const tpl = emails.welcome(name);
        await sendMail({ to: email, subject: tpl.subject, html: tpl.html, userId: user.id, kind: "welcome" });
      } catch {  }
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
      let signupEventId = "";
      try {
        const h = await headers();
        const { eventId } = await sendMetaConversion({
          eventName: "CompleteRegistration",
          email,
          externalId: user.id,
          clientIp: clientIp(h),
          userAgent: h.get("user-agent") ?? undefined,
          fbc: jar.get("_fbc")?.value || undefined,
          fbp: jar.get("_fbp")?.value || undefined,
          sourceUrl: process.env.APP_URL ? `${process.env.APP_URL}/register` : undefined,
        });
        signupEventId = eventId;
      } catch {
        
      }
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
      try {
        const { sendVerificationEmail } = await import("./email-verification");
        await sendVerificationEmail(user);
      } catch (e) {
        console.error("[register] verification email failed:", e);
      }
    
    
    
    
      try {
        const { recordAdsConversion } = await import("./google-ads");
        await recordAdsConversion({ kind: "signup", userId: user.id, dedupeKey: `signup:${user.id}` });
      } catch {
        
      }
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
      const next = safeNext(formData.get("next"));
      const dest = next ?? "/dashboard";
      const marker = `signup=1${signupEventId ? `&se=${encodeURIComponent(signupEventId)}` : ""}`;
      redirect(`${dest}${dest.includes("?") ? "&" : "?"}${marker}`);
    }
    
    export async function login(
      prev: any,
      formData: FormData,
    ): Promise<{ error?: string; needs2fa?: boolean }> {
      const parsed = credsSchema.safeParse({
    
    
    
        email: normalizeEmail(formData.get("email")),
        password: formData.get("password"),
      });
      if (!parsed.success) return { error: "Invalid email or password." };
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
      const rlId = `${parsed.data.email}:${clientIp(await headers())}`;
      const maxAttempts = Math.max(1, await getSettingInt("login_ratelimit_max", 10));
      const rl = checkRateLimit(`login:${rlId}`, { max: maxAttempts, windowMs: 15 * 60 * 1000 });
      if (!rl.ok) return { error: `Too many attempts. Try again in ${rl.retryAfterSec}s.` };
    
      const user = await db.user.findUnique({ where: { email: parsed.data.email } });
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
      const passwordOk = user
        ? await bcrypt.compare(parsed.data.password, user.passwordHash)
        : ((await bcrypt.compare(parsed.data.password, DUMMY_PASSWORD_HASH)), false);
    
    
    
      if (!user || !passwordOk) {
        return { error: "Invalid email or password." };
      }
      if (user.suspended) return { error: "This account is suspended." };
    
      if (user.totpEnabled) {
        const code = String(formData.get("code") ?? "").trim();
        if (!code) return { error: "Enter your 2FA code to continue.", needs2fa: true };
        if (!(await verifyTotpForUser(user.id, code))) {
          return { error: "Invalid 2FA code.", needs2fa: true };
        }
      }
    
      resetRateLimit(`login:${rlId}`);
      await audit(user.id, "user.login");
      await createSession(user.id);
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
      const next = safeNext(formData.get("next"));
      redirect(next ?? roleHome(user.role));
    }
    
    export async function logout() {
      await destroySession();
      redirect("/login");
    }
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    export async function previewOrderCoupon(input: {
      planId: string;
      osTemplateId: string;
      termMonths?: number;
      dedicatedIp?: boolean;
      extraDiskGB?: number;
      couponCode: string;
    }): Promise<{ ok: boolean; label?: string; discountCents?: number; error?: string }> {
      await requireUser();
      const code = String(input.couponCode ?? "").trim().toUpperCase();
      if (!code) return { ok: false, error: "Enter a code." };
      try {
        const quote = await orderQuote({
          planId: input.planId,
          osTemplateId: input.osTemplateId,
          hostname: "preview-host",
          termMonths: Number(input.termMonths ?? 1),
          dedicatedIp: !!input.dedicatedIp,
          extraDiskGB: input.extraDiskGB,
          couponCode: code,
        });
        if (quote.couponError) return { ok: false, error: quote.couponError };
        if (quote.couponDiscountCents > 0) return { ok: true, label: quote.couponLabel, discountCents: quote.couponDiscountCents };
        return { ok: false, error: "That code doesn’t apply to this order." };
      } catch (e) {
        if (e instanceof OrderError) return { ok: false, error: e.message };
        return { ok: false, error: "Couldn’t check that code. Try again." };
      }
    }
    
    
    const RESERVATION_GRACE_MS = 10 * 60 * 1000;
    
    export async function payAndDeploy(
      prev: any,
      formData: FormData,
    ): Promise<{ error?: string; checkout?: EmbeddedCheckout }> {
      const user = await requireUser();
    
    
    
    
    
      try {
        await assertNotImpersonating();
      } catch (e) {
        return { error: e instanceof Error ? e.message : "This action is disabled while impersonating a customer." };
      }
    
    
    
      const unverified = await emailVerificationError(user);
      if (unverified) return { error: unverified };
    
      const limitErr = await serverLimitError(user.id);
      if (limitErr) return { error: limitErr };
      const intent = intentFromFormData(formData);
    
    
    
    
      intent.partnerSource = partnerSourceFromCookie(await cookies());
    
      let quote;
      try {
        quote = await orderQuote(intent, { userId: user.id });
    
        await applyOrderRegion(intent, user.id);
      } catch (e) {
        if (e instanceof OrderError) return { error: e.message };
        throw e;
      }
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
      const promoIpHash = hashIp(clientIp(await headers()), process.env.AUTH_SECRET ?? "nxeon") || null;
      if (quote.promoCreditCents > 0 && !(await firstMonthFreeEligible(user.id, promoIpHash))) {
        quote = await orderQuote(intent, { suppressPromo: true, userId: user.id });
      }
      const promoCampaign = promoCampaignFromCookie(await cookies());
    
      const isAdmin = user.role === "ADMIN";
      const freePath = !!intent.free && isAdmin;
    
    
    
    
      const useWallet = formData.get("useWallet") !== "0";
    
    
    
    
    
    
      const taxCtx = freePath ? { ratePercent: 0, country: "", reverseCharged: false } : await taxContextForUser(user);
      const taxOn = (netCents: number) => Math.round((netCents * taxCtx.ratePercent) / 100);
      const dueWithTax = (netCents: number) => netCents + taxOn(netCents);
    
    
      let { walletApplied, cardCents } = freePath
        ? { walletApplied: 0, cardCents: 0 }
        : walletSplit({ dueCents: dueWithTax(quote.chargeCents), balanceCents: user.balanceCents, useWallet });
    
    
    
    
      if (freePath || cardCents <= 0) {
        let serverId: string;
        try {
    
    
          serverId = await placeOrder(user, intent, null, { ipHash: promoIpHash, campaign: promoCampaign });
        } catch (e) {
          if (e instanceof OrderError) return { error: e.message };
          throw e;
        }
    
    
    
    
        redirect(`/dashboard/servers/${serverId}${freePath ? "" : "?purchase=1"}`);
      }
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
      let promoReservationToken: string | null = null;
      if (quote.promoCreditCents > 0) {
        const reservedUntil = new Date(Date.now() + ORDER_CHECKOUT_EXPIRES_MS + RESERVATION_GRACE_MS);
        const res = await reserveFirstMonthFree({
          userId: user.id,
          ipHash: promoIpHash,
          serviceType: "VPS",
          campaign: promoCampaign,
          chargedCents: quote.chargeCents,
          reservedUntil,
        });
        if (res.reserved) {
          promoReservationToken = res.token;
        } else {
    
    
          quote = await orderQuote(intent, { suppressPromo: true, userId: user.id });
          ({ walletApplied, cardCents } = walletSplit({ dueCents: dueWithTax(quote.chargeCents), balanceCents: user.balanceCents, useWallet }));
    
    
          if (cardCents <= 0) {
            let serverId: string;
            try {
              serverId = await placeOrder(user, intent, null, { ipHash: promoIpHash, campaign: promoCampaign });
            } catch (e) {
              if (e instanceof OrderError) return { error: e.message };
              throw e;
            }
            redirect(`/dashboard/servers/${serverId}?purchase=1`);
          }
        }
      }
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
      let checkoutIntent = intent;
      let couponReservationToken: string | null = null;
      if (quote.couponId && quote.couponDiscountCents > 0) {
        const reservedUntil = new Date(Date.now() + ORDER_CHECKOUT_EXPIRES_MS + RESERVATION_GRACE_MS);
        const res = await reserveOrderCoupon({
          couponId: quote.couponId,
          maxRedemptions: quote.couponMaxRedemptions,
          firstTimeOnly: quote.couponFirstTimeOnly,
          excludeActiveCustomers: quote.couponExcludeActiveCustomers,
          userId: user.id,
          ipHash: promoIpHash,
          amountCents: quote.couponDiscountCents,
          reservedUntil,
        });
        if (res.reserved) {
          couponReservationToken = res.token;
        } else {
    
    
    
    
          checkoutIntent = { ...intent, couponCode: undefined };
          quote = await orderQuote(checkoutIntent, { suppressPromo: !promoReservationToken, userId: user.id });
          ({ walletApplied, cardCents } = walletSplit({ dueCents: dueWithTax(quote.chargeCents), balanceCents: user.balanceCents, useWallet }));
        }
      }
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
      let checkout: EmbeddedCheckout | null;
      try {
    
    
    
    
    
        checkout = await createOrderPaymentSession(
          user,
          cardCents,
          checkoutIntent,
    
    
          orderPriceOf(quote, { taxCents: taxOn(quote.chargeCents), ratePercent: taxCtx.ratePercent, country: taxCtx.country }),
          promoCampaign,
          promoReservationToken,
          couponReservationToken,
        );
      } catch (e) {
        if (promoReservationToken) await releaseReservation(promoReservationToken).catch(() => {});
        if (couponReservationToken) await releaseOrderCouponReservation(couponReservationToken).catch(() => {});
        throw e;
      }
      if (!checkout) {
        if (promoReservationToken) await releaseReservation(promoReservationToken).catch(() => {});
        if (couponReservationToken) await releaseOrderCouponReservation(couponReservationToken).catch(() => {});
        return { error: "Card payments aren't set up yet — top up your wallet to deploy." };
      }
    
      return { checkout };
    }
    
    
    async function ownedServer(serverId: string) {
      const user = await requireUser();
      const server = await db.server.findUnique({ where: { id: serverId } });
      if (!server) throw new Error("Not found");
      if (server.userId !== user.id && user.role !== "ADMIN") throw new Error("Forbidden");
      return { user, server };
    }
    
    export async function serverAction(serverId: string, action: string): Promise<{ error?: string; jobId?: string } | void> {
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
      const { user, server } = await ownedServer(serverId);
      const allowed: Record<string, "START" | "STOP" | "REBOOT" | "REINSTALL" | "DELETE"> = {
        start: "START",
        stop: "STOP",
        reboot: "REBOOT",
        reinstall: "REINSTALL",
        delete: "DELETE",
      };
      const type = allowed[action];
      if (!type) throw new Error("Invalid action"); // not reachable from the UI — a real bug if it happens
      if (server.status === "SUSPENDED" && user.role !== "ADMIN") {
        return { error: "This server is suspended. Settle the outstanding balance to control it again." };
      }
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
      if (type === "REINSTALL") {
        const pinnedVolumes = await db.volume.count({ where: { serverId } });
        if (pinnedVolumes > 0) {
          return {
            error:
              "Delete your data volume(s) before reinstalling — a reinstall wipes the whole server, and that includes detached volumes still assigned to it. Open Volumes first.",
          };
        }
      }
    
    
    
    
    
      const queued = await withServerJobLock(serverId, async (tx) => {
        const pending = await tx.job.count({
          where: { serverId, status: { in: ["QUEUED", "RUNNING"] } },
        });
        if (pending > 0) return null;
        if (type === "DELETE") {
          await tx.server.update({ where: { id: serverId }, data: { status: "DELETING" } });
        }
        return tx.job.create({ data: { type, serverId }, select: { id: true } });
      });
    
    
      if (!queued) return { error: "Another operation is already running on this server. Wait for it to finish." };
    
      await audit(user.id, `server.${action}`, serverId);
      if (type === "DELETE") redirect(user.role === "ADMIN" ? "/admin/servers" : "/dashboard");
      revalidatePath(`/dashboard/servers/${serverId}`);
      return { jobId: queued.id };
    }
    
    
    export async function topUp(prev: any, formData: FormData): Promise<{ error?: string }> {
      const user = await requireUser();
    
    
      await assertNotImpersonating();
    
    
    
      if (process.env.NODE_ENV === "production") {
        return { error: "Please add funds with a card via the secure checkout." };
      }
    
    
      const { stripeConfigured } = await import("./stripe");
      if (stripeConfigured()) {
        return { error: "Please add funds with a card via the secure checkout." };
      }
    
      const topupIp = clientIp(await headers());
      const topupRl = checkRateLimit(`topup:${user.id}:${topupIp}`, { max: 10, windowMs: 60 * 60 * 1000 });
      if (!topupRl.ok) {
        return { error: `Too many top-up attempts. Try again in ${topupRl.retryAfterSec}s.` };
      }
      const amount = Number(formData.get("amount"));
      if (!Number.isFinite(amount) || amount < 1 || amount > 1000) {
        return { error: "Amount must be between $1 and $1000." };
      }
      const cents = Math.round(amount * 100);
    
      const minTopup = await getSettingInt("min_topup_cents", 500);
      if (cents < minTopup) {
        return { error: `Minimum top-up is $${(minTopup / 100).toFixed(2)}.` };
      }
    
    
      const jar = await cookies();
      const couponCode = jar.get(COUPON_COOKIE)?.value?.trim();
      let bonusCents = 0;
      let couponId: string | null = null;
      let couponLabel = "";
      if (couponCode) {
        const result = await applyCouponToTopup(couponCode, cents);
        if (result.ok) {
          bonusCents = result.bonusCents;
          couponId = result.couponId;
          couponLabel = result.label;
        } else {
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
          return {
            error:
              `${result.error ?? "That promo code can't be applied to this amount."} ` +
              `Nothing has been charged — adjust the amount or remove the code and try again.`,
          };
        }
      }
    
      try {
        await db.$transaction(async (tx) => {
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
          if (bonusCents > 0 && couponId) {
            const c = await tx.coupon.findUnique({ where: { id: couponId }, select: { maxRedemptions: true } });
            const claim = await tx.coupon.updateMany({
              where:
                c?.maxRedemptions == null
                  ? { id: couponId, active: true }
                  : { id: couponId, active: true, timesRedeemed: { lt: c.maxRedemptions } },
              data: { timesRedeemed: { increment: 1 } },
            });
            if (claim.count !== 1) throw new CouponClaimError("That code has just reached its redemption limit.");
            try {
              await tx.couponRedemption.create({
                data: { couponId, userId: user.id, amountCents: bonusCents, context: "topup" },
              });
            } catch {
              throw new CouponClaimError("You’ve already used this code.");
            }
          }
          await tx.user.update({
            where: { id: user.id },
            data: { balanceCents: { increment: cents + bonusCents } },
          });
          await tx.walletTransaction.create({
            data: { userId: user.id, amountCents: cents, description: "Wallet top-up (demo gateway)" },
          });
          if (bonusCents > 0 && couponId) {
            await tx.walletTransaction.create({
              data: {
                userId: user.id,
                amountCents: bonusCents,
                description: `Promo ${couponCode!.toUpperCase()} — ${couponLabel}`,
              },
            });
          }
        });
      } catch (e) {
    
    
        if (e instanceof CouponClaimError) {
          return { error: `${e.message} Nothing has been charged — remove the code and try again.` };
        }
        throw e;
      }
      jar.delete(COUPON_COOKIE);
      await audit(user.id, "wallet.topup", `$${amount}${bonusCents > 0 ? ` +bonus $${(bonusCents / 100).toFixed(2)}` : ""}`);
    
      try {
        await maybeRewardReferral(user.id);
      } catch {  }
      revalidatePath("/dashboard/billing");
      return {};
    }
    
    
    export async function toggleAutoRenew(serverId: string) {
      const { user, server } = await ownedServer(serverId);
      if (server.isFree) return;
      await db.server.update({
        where: { id: serverId },
        data: { autoRenew: !server.autoRenew },
      });
      await audit(user.id, "server.autorenew_toggle", `${serverId} -> ${!server.autoRenew}`);
      revalidatePath(`/dashboard/servers/${serverId}`);
    }
    
    export async function renewNow(serverId: string): Promise<{ error?: string } | void> {
    
      await assertNotImpersonating();
      const { user, server } = await ownedServer(serverId);
      if (server.isFree || !server.expiresAt) return;
    
    
    
    
    
      const unverified = await emailVerificationError(user);
      if (unverified) return { error: unverified };
      const plan = await db.plan.findUnique({ where: { id: server.planId } });
      if (!plan) return;
    
    
    
    
      const gamePlan = server.gamePlanId
        ? await db.gamePlan.findUnique({
            where: { id: server.gamePlanId },
            select: { name: true, priceCents: true, termMonths: true },
          })
        : null;
    
    
      const hasDedicatedIp = !!(await db.dedicatedIp.findFirst({
        where: { serverId }, select: { id: true },
      }));
    
    
      const owner = await db.user.findUnique({ where: { id: server.userId } });
      if (!owner) return;
      const renewTaxCtx = await taxContextForUser(owner);
    
    
    
      const partnerPct = (await partnerDiscountForRenewal(server.userId)).percent;
    
    
    
    
      const quote = renewalQuote(
        {
          termMonths: server.termMonths,
          extraDiskGB: server.extraDiskGB,
          plan: { name: plan.name, priceCents: plan.priceCents },
          gamePlan,
          dedicatedIp: hasDedicatedIp ? {} : null,
          wantsDedicatedIp: server.wantsDedicatedIp,
          backupTier: server.backupTier,
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
          annualDiscountPct: server.annualDiscountPct,
          grandfatheredRenewalCents: server.grandfatheredRenewalCents,
    
    
    
    
    
          pendingRenewalDiscountPercent: server.pendingRenewalDiscountPercent,
        },
        {
          ipMonthlyCents: hasDedicatedIp ? await dedicatedIpMonthlyCents() : 0,
          extraDiskPerGbCents: await extraDiskPerGbCents(),
          backupPlusCents: await backupPlusMonthlyCents(),
          annualDiscountPct: await annualDiscountPct(),
    
    
          taxRatePercent: renewTaxCtx.ratePercent,
          partnerDiscountPercent: partnerPct,
        },
      );
      const renewCents = quote.totalCents;
      const term = { months: quote.months };
      const extraDiskMonthly = await extraDiskMonthlyCents(server.extraDiskGB);
      const extraDiskCents = gamePlan ? 0 : extraDiskMonthly * quote.months;
    
    
    
    
      const preRetentionNetCents = quote.netCents + quote.retentionDiscountCents + quote.partnerDiscountCents;
      if (owner.balanceCents < renewCents) {
        return { error: "Insufficient balance — top up your wallet first." };
      }
    
    
      try {
        await assertSpendCap(owner, renewCents);
      } catch (e) {
        if (e instanceof SpendCapError) return { error: e.message };
        throw e;
      }
    
    
    
    
      const E = server.expiresAt;
      const base = new Date(Math.max(E.getTime(), Date.now()));
      const newExpiry = addMonths(base, term.months);
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
      const renewedPercent = prorateBlendPercent({
        currentPercent: resolveTermPromoPercent(server.termPromoPercent),
        currentMonths: remainingMonths(E, Date.now()),
        addedPercent: 0,
        addedMonths: term.months,
      });
      try {
        await db.$transaction(async (tx) => {
    
    
          const adv = await tx.server.updateMany({
            where: { id: serverId, expiresAt: E },
            data: {
              expiresAt: newExpiry,
              termPromoPercent: renewedPercent,
              suspendedNote: null,
              suspendReason: null,
    
    
    
    
              pendingRenewalDiscountPercent: null,
            },
          });
          if (adv.count === 0) throw new AlreadyRenewedError();
    
          await debitWallet(tx, server.userId, renewCents);
          await tx.walletTransaction.create({
            data: {
              userId: server.userId,
              amountCents: -renewCents,
              description: `Renewal: ${quote.label} (${server.hostname}) — ${quote.durationLabel}`,
            },
          });
    
    
          await tx.invoice.create({
            data: invoiceCreateData({
              userId: server.userId,
              description: `Renewal: ${quote.label} (${server.hostname}) — ${quote.durationLabel}`,
              status: "PAID",
    
              taxCents: quote.taxCents,
              taxRatePercent: quote.taxRatePercent,
              taxCountry: renewTaxCtx.country,
              dedicatedIpCents: quote.ipCents,
              period: { start: E, end: newExpiry },
              lineItems: [
                {
                  description: `${quote.label} (${server.hostname}) — ${quote.durationLabel}`,
                  qty: 1,
                  unitCents: preRetentionNetCents - extraDiskCents,
                  amountCents: preRetentionNetCents - extraDiskCents,
                },
                ...(extraDiskCents > 0
                  ? [
                      {
                        description: `${extraDiskLabel(server.extraDiskGB)} — ${server.hostname} (permanent, $${(extraDiskMonthly / 100).toFixed(2)}/mo)`,
                        qty: 1,
                        unitCents: extraDiskCents,
                        amountCents: extraDiskCents,
                      },
                    ]
                  : []),
                ...(quote.retentionDiscountCents > 0
                  ? [
                      {
                        description: `Retention offer — 50% off this renewal (${server.hostname})`,
                        qty: 1,
                        unitCents: -quote.retentionDiscountCents,
                        amountCents: -quote.retentionDiscountCents,
                      },
                    ]
                  : []),
                ...(quote.partnerDiscountCents > 0
                  ? [
                      {
                        description: `Partner discount — ${quote.partnerDiscountPercent}% off (${server.hostname})`,
                        qty: 1,
                        unitCents: -quote.partnerDiscountCents,
                        amountCents: -quote.partnerDiscountCents,
                      },
                    ]
                  : []),
              ],
            }),
          });
        });
      } catch (e) {
        if (e instanceof InsufficientBalanceError) return { error: "Insufficient balance — top up your wallet first." };
        if (e instanceof AlreadyRenewedError) return { error: "This server was just renewed — refresh to see the new expiry." };
        throw e;
      }
      await audit(user.id, "server.renew_now", serverId);
    
      await maybeAlertBudget(server.userId);
    
      await accrueRecentCommissionsForCustomer(server.userId);
    
    
    
    
    
      if (server.status === "SUSPENDED" && server.suspendReason === "UNPAID") {
        const pending = await db.job.count({
          where: { serverId, status: { in: ["QUEUED", "RUNNING"] } },
        });
        if (pending === 0) await db.job.create({ data: { type: "UNSUSPEND", serverId } });
      }
      revalidatePath(`/dashboard/servers/${serverId}`);
    }
    
    
    export async function adminExtendServer(prev: any, formData: FormData): Promise<{ error?: string }> {
      const admin = await requireAdmin();
      const serverId = String(formData.get("serverId") ?? "");
      const days = Number(formData.get("days"));
      if (!serverId || !Number.isFinite(days) || days === 0 || Math.abs(days) > 365) {
        return { error: "Days must be between -365 and 365." };
      }
      const server = await db.server.findUnique({ where: { id: serverId } });
      if (!server) return { error: "Server not found." };
      const base = server.expiresAt ? server.expiresAt.getTime() : Date.now();
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
      const gifted = days > 0;
      const termPromoPercent = gifted
        ? prorateBlendPercent({
            currentPercent: resolveTermPromoPercent(server.termPromoPercent),
            currentMonths: remainingMonths(server.expiresAt, Date.now()),
            addedPercent: 100, // gifted: no cash changed hands for these months
            addedMonths: (days * 86400_000) / MS_PER_AVG_MONTH,
          })
        : resolveTermPromoPercent(server.termPromoPercent);
    
      await db.server.update({
        where: { id: serverId },
        data: { expiresAt: new Date(base + days * 86400_000), termPromoPercent },
      });
      await audit(
        admin.id,
        "admin.extend_server",
        `${serverId} ${days}d${gifted ? ` gifted (rate basis ${server.termPromoPercent ?? "null"}% -> ${termPromoPercent}% off)` : ""}`,
      );
      revalidatePath("/admin/subscriptions");
      return {};
    }
    
    
    export async function adminSetGrandfatheredPrice(prev: any, formData: FormData): Promise<{ error?: string }> {
      const admin = await requireAdmin();
      const serverId = String(formData.get("serverId") ?? "");
      const raw = String(formData.get("priceDollars") ?? "").trim();
      const server = await db.server.findUnique({ where: { id: serverId } });
      if (!server) return { error: "Server not found." };
    
      const dollars = Number(raw);
    
    
    
      if (!raw || !Number.isFinite(dollars) || dollars < 0 || dollars > 100_000) {
        return { error: "Enter a valid monthly price between $0 and $100,000." };
      }
      const cents = Math.round(dollars * 100);
      const before = server.grandfatheredRenewalCents;
    
      await db.server.update({ where: { id: serverId }, data: { grandfatheredRenewalCents: cents } });
      await audit(
        admin.id,
        "admin.set_grandfathered_price",
        `${serverId} (${server.hostname}) ${before != null ? `$${(before / 100).toFixed(2)}/mo` : "live catalog price"} -> $${(cents / 100).toFixed(2)}/mo locked`,
      );
      revalidatePath(`/admin/servers/${serverId}`);
      revalidatePath("/admin/servers");
      revalidatePath("/admin/subscriptions");
      return {};
    }
    
    
    export async function adminClearGrandfatheredPrice(serverId: string) {
      const admin = await requireAdmin();
      const server = await db.server.findUnique({ where: { id: serverId }, select: { hostname: true, grandfatheredRenewalCents: true } });
      if (!server) return;
      await db.server.update({ where: { id: serverId }, data: { grandfatheredRenewalCents: null } });
      await audit(
        admin.id,
        "admin.clear_grandfathered_price",
        `${serverId} (${server.hostname}) cleared${server.grandfatheredRenewalCents != null ? ` (was $${(server.grandfatheredRenewalCents / 100).toFixed(2)}/mo)` : ""} -> live catalog price`,
      );
      revalidatePath(`/admin/servers/${serverId}`);
      revalidatePath("/admin/servers");
      revalidatePath("/admin/subscriptions");
    }
    
    export type AdminRenewNowResult =
      | {
          ok: true;
          
          path: "wallet" | "wallet+card" | "card";
          walletCents: number;
          cardCents: number;
          totalCents: number;
          
          newExpiry: string;
        }
      | { ok: false; error: string; code?: string };
    
    
    export async function adminRenewServerNow(serverId: string): Promise<AdminRenewNowResult> {
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
      const admin = await requireAdmin();
    
      const server = await db.server.findUnique({
        where: { id: serverId },
        include: { plan: true, user: true, gamePlan: true, dedicatedIp: true },
      });
      if (!server) return { ok: false, error: "Server not found." };
      if (server.isFree || !server.expiresAt) {
        return { ok: false, error: "This server is free or has no billing period — there is nothing to renew." };
      }
    
    
    
    
      const pending = await db.job.count({
        where: { serverId, status: { in: ["QUEUED", "RUNNING"] } },
      });
      if (pending > 0) {
        return { ok: false, error: "Another operation is already running on this server. Wait for it to finish." };
      }
    
    
      const taxCtx = await taxContextForUser(server.user);
    
    
      const prices = {
        ipMonthlyCents: await dedicatedIpMonthlyCents(),
        extraDiskPerGbCents: await extraDiskPerGbCents(),
        backupPlusCents: await backupPlusMonthlyCents(),
        annualDiscountPct: await annualDiscountPct(),
      };
      const cfg = await dunningConfig();
    
      const result = await renewServerImmediately(server, {
        now: new Date(),
        cfg,
        prices,
        taxRatePercent: taxCtx.ratePercent,
        taxCountry: taxCtx.country,
      });
    
      if (!result.ok) {
        await audit(
          admin.id,
          "admin.renew_now",
          `${serverId} (${server.hostname}) FAILED — ${result.reason}: ${result.message}`,
        );
        return { ok: false, error: result.message, code: result.code };
      }
    
      await audit(
        admin.id,
        "admin.renew_now",
        `${serverId} (${server.hostname}) renewed via ${result.path} — wallet $${(result.walletCents / 100).toFixed(2)} + card $${(result.cardCents / 100).toFixed(2)} = $${(result.totalCents / 100).toFixed(2)}, new expiry ${result.newExpiry.toISOString().slice(0, 10)}`,
      );
    
    
    
      await maybeAlertBudget(server.userId);
      await accrueRecentCommissionsForCustomer(server.userId);
    
    
    
    
      if (server.status === "SUSPENDED" && server.suspendReason === "UNPAID") {
        const pendingUnsuspend = await db.job.count({
          where: { serverId, status: { in: ["QUEUED", "RUNNING"] } },
        });
        if (pendingUnsuspend === 0) await db.job.create({ data: { type: "UNSUSPEND", serverId } });
      }
    
      revalidatePath(`/admin/servers/${serverId}`);
      revalidatePath("/admin/servers");
      revalidatePath("/admin/subscriptions");
    
      return {
        ok: true,
        path: result.path,
        walletCents: result.walletCents,
        cardCents: result.cardCents,
        totalCents: result.totalCents,
        newExpiry: result.newExpiry.toISOString(),
      };
    }
    
    export async function adminSetServerFree(serverId: string, isFree: boolean) {
      const admin = await requireAdmin();
      await db.server.update({
        where: { id: serverId },
        data: {

        },
      });

      revalidatePath("/admin/subscriptions");
    }
    
    export async function adminGrantFreeVps(prev: any, formData: FormData): Promise<{ error?: string }> {
      const admin = await requireAdmin();
      const parsed = z
        .object({
          userId: z.string().min(1),
          planId: z.string().min(1),
          osTemplateId: z.string().min(1),
          hostname: z.string().min(3).max(40).regex(/^[a-z0-9]([a-z0-9-]*[a-z0-9])?$/i),
        })
        .safeParse({
          userId: formData.get("userId"),
          planId: formData.get("planId"),
          osTemplateId: formData.get("osTemplateId"),
          hostname: formData.get("hostname"),
        });
      if (!parsed.success) return { error: "Invalid input (hostname: letters/digits/hyphens, 3-40 chars)." };
    
      const target = await db.user.findUnique({ where: { id: parsed.data.userId } });
      if (!target) return { error: "User not found." };
    
    
    
    
    
    
      const normalizedHostname = normalizeHostname(parsed.data.hostname);
      if (await isHostnameTaken(normalizedHostname)) {
        return { error: "That hostname is already in use by another server — pick another." };
      }
    
      let server;
      try {
        server = await db.server.create({
          data: {
            name: normalizedHostname,
            hostname: normalizedHostname,
            userId: target.id,
            planId: parsed.data.planId,
            osTemplateId: parsed.data.osTemplateId,
            isFree: true,
    
    
    
    
            termPromoPercent: 100,
          },
        });
      } catch (e) {
        if (isHostnameConflict(e)) return { error: "That hostname was just taken by another server — pick another." };
        throw e;
      }
      await db.job.create({ data: { type: "PROVISION", serverId: server.id } });
      await audit(admin.id, "admin.grant_free_vps", `user=${target.email} server=${server.id}`);
      redirect(`/admin/servers`);
    }
    
    export async function adminSuspendServer(serverId: string, suspend: boolean) {
      const admin = await requireAdmin();
    
    
    
    
    
    
      await db.server.update({
        where: { id: serverId },
        data: suspend
          ? { suspendReason: "ADMIN", suspendedNote: "suspended by support — contact us to restore access" }
          : { suspendReason: null, suspendedNote: null },
      });
      await db.job.create({ data: { type: suspend ? "SUSPEND" : "UNSUSPEND", serverId } });
      await audit(admin.id, suspend ? "admin.suspend_server" : "admin.unsuspend_server", serverId);
      revalidatePath("/admin/servers");
    }
    
    
    export async function reconcileServerFromProxmox(
      serverId: string
    ): Promise<{ ok?: true; status?: string; message?: string; error?: string }> {
      const admin = await requireArea("fleet");
    
      const server = await db.server.findUnique({
        where: { id: serverId },
        include: {
          node: true,
          user: { select: { id: true, email: true } },
          plan: { select: { name: true } },
        },
      });
      if (!server) return { error: "Server not found." };
      if (!server.vmid || !server.node) {
        return { error: "This server has no VM on Proxmox yet — use Retry/Delete, not reconcile." };
      }
    
      const node = server.node;
      const client = pveFor({
        apiUrl: node.apiUrl,
        pveNode: node.pveNode,
        tokenId: node.tokenId,
        tokenSecret: node.tokenSecret,
      });
    
      let vmState: string | undefined;
      try {
        const s = await client.vmStatus(server.vmid);
        vmState = typeof s?.status === "string" ? s.status : undefined;
      } catch {
        return { error: "Couldn't reach the hypervisor — try again." };
      }
    
      const prev = server.status;
    
      if (vmState === "running") {
        await db.server.update({
          where: { id: server.id },
          data: { status: "RUNNING", provisionError: null },
        });
    
        await db.job.create({ data: { type: "SYNC_FORWARDS", serverId: server.id } });
    
    
    
        if (prev === "ERROR" || prev === "PENDING" || prev === "PROVISIONING") {
          await sendServerDeployedEmail(server, server.user);
        }
    
        await audit(admin.id, "server.reconcile", `${server.hostname} ${server.vmid} → RUNNING`);
        revalidatePath("/admin/servers");
        revalidatePath(`/admin/servers/${server.id}`);
        return { ok: true, status: "RUNNING" };
      }
    
      if (vmState === "stopped") {
        await db.server.update({
          where: { id: server.id },
          data: { status: "STOPPED", provisionError: null },
        });
        await audit(admin.id, "server.reconcile", `${server.hostname} ${server.vmid} → STOPPED`);
        revalidatePath("/admin/servers");
        revalidatePath(`/admin/servers/${server.id}`);
        return { ok: true, status: "STOPPED" };
      }
    
    
      await audit(admin.id, "server.reconcile", `${server.hostname} ${server.vmid} → no-op (${vmState ?? "unknown"})`);
      return {
        message: `The VM reports "${vmState ?? "unknown"}"; status left unchanged. Try again once it settles.`,
      };
    }
    
    export async function adminSetUserSuspended(userId: string, suspended: boolean) {
      const admin = await requireAdmin();
      if (userId === admin.id) throw new Error("Cannot suspend yourself");
      await db.user.update({ where: { id: userId }, data: { suspended } });
      await audit(admin.id, suspended ? "admin.suspend_user" : "admin.unsuspend_user", userId);
      revalidatePath("/admin/users");
    }

      const admin = await requireAdmin();
      await db.user.update({ where: { id: userId }, data: { excludeFromRevenue: excluded } });
      await audit(admin.id, excluded ? "admin.revenue_exclude" : "admin.revenue_include", userId);
      revalidatePath("/admin/users");
      revalidatePath("/admin");
      revalidatePath("/admin/analytics");
    }
    
    export async function adminCreditUser(prev: any, formData: FormData): Promise<{ error?: string }> {
      const admin = await requireAdmin();
      const userId = String(formData.get("userId") ?? "");
      const amount = Number(formData.get("amount"));
      if (!userId || !Number.isFinite(amount) || amount === 0 || Math.abs(amount) > 10000) {
        return { error: "Invalid amount." };
      }
      const cents = Math.round(amount * 100);
      await db.$transaction([
        db.user.update({ where: { id: userId }, data: { balanceCents: { increment: cents } } }),
        db.walletTransaction.create({
          data: { userId, amountCents: cents, description: `Admin credit adjustment` },
        }),
      ]);
      await audit(admin.id, "admin.credit_user", `user=${userId} $${amount}`);
      revalidatePath("/admin/users");
      return {};
    }
    
    
    
    export async function adminAddCustomerNote(
      prev: any,
      formData: FormData,
    ): Promise<{ error?: string }> {
      const admin = await requireAdmin();
      const userId = String(formData.get("userId") ?? "");
      const body = String(formData.get("body") ?? "").trim();
      if (!userId) return { error: "Missing customer." };
      if (!body) return { error: "Note cannot be empty." };
      if (body.length > 5000) return { error: "Note is too long (5000 char max)." };
      const customer = await db.user.findUnique({ where: { id: userId }, select: { id: true } });
      if (!customer) return { error: "Customer not found." };
      await db.customerNote.create({ data: { userId, authorId: admin.id, body } });
      await audit(admin.id, "admin.add_customer_note", `user=${userId}`);
      revalidatePath(`/admin/users/${userId}`);
      return {};
    }
    
    
    export async function adminEditCustomerNote(
      prev: any,
      formData: FormData,
    ): Promise<{ error?: string }> {
      const admin = await requireAdmin();
      const noteId = String(formData.get("noteId") ?? "");
      const body = String(formData.get("body") ?? "").trim();
      if (!noteId) return { error: "Missing note." };
      if (!body) return { error: "Note cannot be empty." };
      if (body.length > 5000) return { error: "Note is too long (5000 char max)." };
      const note = await db.customerNote.findUnique({ where: { id: noteId }, select: { userId: true } });
      if (!note) return { error: "Note not found." };
      await db.customerNote.update({ where: { id: noteId }, data: { body } });
      await audit(admin.id, "admin.edit_customer_note", `user=${note.userId} note=${noteId}`);
      revalidatePath(`/admin/users/${note.userId}`);
      return {};
    }
    
    
    export async function adminDeleteCustomerNote(noteId: string) {
      const admin = await requireAdmin();
      const note = await db.customerNote.findUnique({ where: { id: noteId }, select: { userId: true } });
      if (!note) return;
      await db.customerNote.delete({ where: { id: noteId } });
      await audit(admin.id, "admin.delete_customer_note", `user=${note.userId} note=${noteId}`);
      revalidatePath(`/admin/users/${note.userId}`);
    }
    
    export async function adminSavePlan(prev: any, formData: FormData): Promise<{ error?: string }> {
      const admin = await requireAdmin();
      const parsed = z
        .object({
          id: z.string().optional(),
          slug: z.string().min(1).max(30).regex(/^[a-z0-9-]+$/),
          name: z.string().min(1).max(50),
          cpuCores: z.coerce.number().int().min(1).max(8),
          ramMB: z.coerce.number().int().min(256).max(49152),
          diskGB: z.coerce.number().int().min(5).max(300),
          bandwidthTB: z.coerce.number().min(0.1).max(100),
          priceCents: z.coerce.number().int().min(0),
    
    
          introPriceCents: z.coerce.number().int().min(0).optional(),
          introTermMonths: z.coerce.number().int().min(1).max(36).optional(),
          active: z.coerce.boolean(),
        })
        .safeParse({
          id: formData.get("id") || undefined,
          slug: formData.get("slug"),
          name: formData.get("name"),
          cpuCores: formData.get("cpuCores"),
          ramMB: formData.get("ramMB"),
          diskGB: formData.get("diskGB"),
          bandwidthTB: formData.get("bandwidthTB"),
          priceCents: formData.get("priceCents"),
    
    
          introPriceCents: formData.get("introPriceCents") || undefined,
          introTermMonths: formData.get("introTermMonths") || undefined,
          active: formData.get("active") === "on",
        });
      if (!parsed.success) return { error: parsed.error.issues[0]?.message ?? "Invalid plan." };
      const { id, introPriceCents, introTermMonths, ...data } = parsed.data;
    
    
      if (introPriceCents != null && introPriceCents >= data.priceCents) {
        return { error: "Intro price must be below the regular price." };
      }
    
    
      const intro = {
        introPriceCents: introPriceCents ?? null,
        introTermMonths: introPriceCents == null ? null : introTermMonths ?? null,
      };
      if (id) {
        await db.plan.update({
          where: { id },
          data: { ...data, ...intro } as Prisma.PlanUncheckedUpdateInput,
        });
      } else {
        await db.plan.create({ data: { ...data, ...intro } as Prisma.PlanUncheckedCreateInput });
      }
      await audit(admin.id, "admin.save_plan", data.slug);
      revalidatePath("/admin/plans");
      redirect("/admin/plans");
    }
